THIS AGREEMENT is entered into on this theday of October, 2020.

BETWEEN

The Governor of Uttar Pradesh represented by Noida International Airport Limited and having its Principal office at office no, 1-15 block P-2-sector Omega -1, Greater Noids, Gautam Buddha Nagar, nar Pradesh (hereinafter referred to as the “Authority” which expression shall unless repugnant t© the context or meaning thereof, include is administrators, successors and assign)of the One Pat;

AND

WHEREAS:

A. Government of Utar Pradesh had procured the in-principle approval from Goverament of India

(GOI) (hereinalier referred to as the “In-Principle Approval) for the establishment of an international airport for public use in Non of Jewar village, District Gautam Buddh Nagar, in the State of Uttar Pradesh (herein referred tos the “Aiport, subjctto the tems and conditions stipulated inthe in-Principle Approval and had resolved to establish the Aieport on design build, finance, operate and transfer (the “DBFOT”) bass in accordance with the tems and conditions to be set forth ina concession agreement BL The Government of Uttar Pradesh (*GoUP") vide its Government Resolution No. 2533 dated 12th December 2018 (“GolIP Approval), approved the implementation of the Project and assigned Noida Intemational Airport Limited as Authority. (C. Based upon the representations and submissions made by the Authority, the Applicable Permits that are required prior tothe implementation ofthe Project were granted tothe Authority from the concerned Government Instrumentalites, . The Authority, had prescribed the technical and commercial terms and conditions, and invited Bids (‘the Request for Qualification cum Proposal” of “RFQ cum REP for development, ‘operation and maintenance of the Airport on DBFOT basis, and invited applications by i Request for Qualification cum Proposal No. NIAL-2/44/2019 dated 25% September 2019, as cum Proposal” of “RPQ cum amended from time to time (cll tively, "Request for Qualification

REP”),

E. After evaluation ofthe Bids received, the Authority had accepted the Bid of the Selected hide, iter alia, the selected bidder Zurich Airport International AG. Authority issued its Leter of ‘Award No, NIAL-02/62/2010 dated 4* May 2020 (hereinaltercalled the “LOA") tothe Selected bidder: and subsequent tothe GoUP GO number 1418%Chappan-2020-31/2016 (PMIC) dated 14* ‘August 2020 requiring inter ali, the execution of this Concession Agreement by 15! October 2020.

BP. The selected bidder has since promoted and incorporated the Concessionaire asa limited lability ‘company under the Companies Act 2013. and has requested the Authority to accept the ‘Concessionare as the entity which shall undertake and perform the obligations and exercise the “4

rights of the Selected bidder under the LOA. including the obligation to enter into this Concession Agreement pursuant tothe LOA for undertaking the Project.

G. By its leter dated June 22020, the Concessionaize has also joined in the said request of the selected bidderto the Authorityto accep ta the enlty which shall undertake and perform the ‘obligations and exercise the rights ofthe selected bidder including the obligation to enter nto this ‘Concession Agreement pursuant tothe LOA. The Concessionaire has further represented tothe effect that it has been promoted by the selected bidder! Consortium forthe purposes hereof.

H The Authority has agreed tothe said request of the selected bidder and the Concessionaite, and has accordingly agreed to enter into this Concession Agreement with the Concessionare for implementation ofthe Project on DBFOT basis, subject to andon the terms and conditions set forth hereinatcr.

ARTICLES

3. GRANT OF CONCESSION

‘A. The Concession,

3.11. ARTICLE 16

16, CHANGE OF SCOPE

16.1.Change ofSeope I6.L The Authority may, notwithstanding anything tothe contrary contained in this Agreement, but subject to the provisions of Clause 16.14, require the provision of additional works and services which are not included in the Master Plan asset out in Schedle A and are necessary for the performance ofthe Aeronautical Services, shall be considered as change of scope (the ‘Change of Scope"). Force Majeure ‘As wed in this Agreement, the expression “Force Majeure” or “Force Majeure Event shall save and ‘excepts expressly provided otherwise, mean occurrencei Indiaof any or all of Non-Poltical Event, Indirect Political Event and Political Event, as defined in Clauses 39.2, 39.3 and 39.4 respectively. if itafects dhe performance by the Party claiming the benefit of Force Majeure (the “Atfected Patty") of is obligations under ths Agreement and which ator event (a) is beyond the reasonable control of the Affected Party, and (b) the Affected Party could not have prevented or overcome by exercise ‘of due diligence and following Good Industry Practice, and (c) has Material Adverse Effect on the Affected Paty 392, Non-Political Event ‘A Non-Political Event shall mean one of more of the following acts oF events:

(a) act of God, epidemic, extremely adverse weather conditions, lightning, earthquake, landslide, cyclone, flood, voleanic eruption, chemical or radioactive contamination or ionising radiation ire or explosion (tothe extent of contamination o radiation or fire or ‘explosion originating from a source external to the Site)

‘SAFETY REQUIREMENTS

|. Guiding principles

(2) Safety Requirements aim at reduction in injuries, loss of life and damage to property resulting from accidents on or about the Aiport irespective of the person(s) a fault.

(b) Users of the Airport include staff of the Concessionare and its contractors working on the Aiport

(6) Safety Requirements apply to all Phases of construction, operation and maintenance with emphasis on identification of factors associated with accidents, consideration of the same, and ‘implementation of appropriate remedial measures. (@) Safety Requirements include measures associated with safe movement, safety management, safety equipment, fire safety enforcement and emergency response, with particular reference to the Safety Guidelines specified in Annex- Lof this Schedule-M.

2. Obligations ofthe Concessionaire The Concessions shll abide by the following insofar as they relate to safety ofthe Users

(a) Applicable Laws and Applicable Permits

(b) Manual for Safety. approved by the Authority

(6) provisions of this Agreement,

(4) relevant ICAO Documents and Annexes, applicable guidelines of DGCA and other relevant Standards Guidelines contained in internationally accepted codes: and

(6) Good Industry Practice

3.1. Forcatrying out safety audit ofthe Airport under and in accordance with this Schedule-M, the ‘Authority shall appoint from time to time, one or more qualified firms or organisations as its consultants (the “Safety Consultant). The Safety Consultant shall employ a team comprising, ‘without limitation, one aeport safety expert and one traffic planner to undertake safety audit of the Aiport.

32. Once in every Accounting Year, safety suit shall be carried out by the Safety Consultant. It shall review, compile and analyse the annual report and accident data ofthe preceding year, and undertake an inspection ofthe Airport. The Safety Consultant shall complete the safety audit within period of! (one) month and submit a Safety Report the "Safety Report) recommending specific improvements if any, equired to be made to the Airport. The Safety Report shall be submitted to the Authority. in $ (five) copies. One copy cach of the Safety Repost shall be forwarded by the ‘Authority to the Concessionare and the Independent Engincer forthwith

4. Safety measures during Opera 1 Period

4.1 The Concessionare shall develop, implement and administer surveillance and safety programme for Users, including responseto hijacking of airraf, correction of safety violations and deficiencies and all other actions necessary to provide a safe environment in accordance with this Agreement

42 The Concessionaire shall establish an Airport Safety Management Unit (the “ASMU")to be functional on and ater COD, and designate one ofits officers tobe in-charge ofthe ASMU, Such officer shall have specialist knowledge and training in airport system safety by having attended a course conducted by a reputed organisation on the subject.

‘TRAFFIC SAMPLING

‘Traffic sampling The Authority may, in its discretion and a its own cost, undertake trafic sampling, pursuant to (Clause 25.4, in order to determine the actual passenger, vehicular, eargo and visitor traffic on the Airport. Such traffic sampling shall he undertaken through the Independent Engineer in the manner ‘sot forth below orn such manner as the Parties hereto may agree upon, ‘Manual trafic count The Independent Engineer shall employ the required number of enumerators who shall count, lasify and record all the Users as they pass by and divide the survey into fixe time periods. The count stations shall be located near the entry gates or atthe fee collection points. The survey shall bbe conducted continuously fora minimum of 24 (twenty four) hours and maximum of 7 (seven) days ata time. Special surveys may also be undertaken for determining the Peak Hour Traffic and such surveys may be carried out for upto

4 (our) Peak Hours ina day fora continuous period of 7 (seven) days. The count period shall be 15 (fifteen) minutes with results summarised hourly. All surveys shall be conducted substantially in the manner specified in this Schedule—Por in such manneras the Parties may agree upon.

Automate traffic count For automatic talfc count to be conductedon intermittent (non-continuous) basis, the Independent Enginccr shall wse suitable and standardised equipment to classify and record the Users passing through the entryor Fee collection points. For this purpose, the counter shall he checked with at least 1,000 (one thousand) Users to ensue that Users are being counted and classified correctly ‘Variation between manual and automatic count [Average Daily Traffic (ADT) shall he determined separately by the aforesaid two methods and in ‘the event thatthe number of Users, as counted by the manual method, varies by more than 1% (one per cent) of the umber of such Users as counted by the automatic method, the manual and automatic count shal be repeated, and inthe event of any discrepancy between the two counts inthe second ‘enumeration, the average there shall be deemed tobe the actual traffic. For the avoidance of doubt, itis expressly agreed tha! the Authority may, in consultation with the Concessionaire, adopt modified ‘or altemative processes of traffic sampling for improving the reliability of such sampling. and may. instead of carrying out Manual ad Avtonutic trafic counts, rely only on Automatic trafic counts ‘or any substitute thereot

i.
